451.29065pt})=\frac{1}{\widetilde{x}_{j}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}|\widetilde{x}_{j}-x^{(i)}_{n,j}|,

(33)

for
j=1,\ldots,k
.
The
j
th
parameter estimate
(at time
n
), for
j=1,\ldots,k
, is given by

\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}x_{n,j}^{(i)}.

(34)

Appendix E
Derivation of the State Error

Here is the derivation of the formula for the state error
\mathit{Err}(\hbox{\hskip 451.29065pt\hskip-29.30621pt\hbox{$(\nu_{n}\odot\mu_{n})(h_{n})$}\hskip-29.30621pt\hskip-451.29065pt\raisebox{18.50012pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-3.34055pt}{\resizebox{53617400}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14236pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.98pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14285pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-451.29065pt\hskip 451.29065pt})
from Section
4.3
.

\displaystyle\;\mathit{Err}(\hbox{\hskip 451.29065pt\hskip-29.30621pt\hbox{$(\nu_{n}\odot\mu_{n})(h_{n})$}\hskip-29.30621pt\hskip-451.29065pt\raisebox{18.50012pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-3.34055pt}{\resizebox{53617400}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14236pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.98pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14285pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-451.29065pt\hskip 451.29065pt})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{L}\sum_{k=1}^{L}\int_{S}\lambda y.\rho_{k}(\widetilde{y}_{n}^{(k)},y)\;d\hbox{\hskip 451.29065pt\hskip-35.03308pt\hbox{$(\nu_{n}\odot\mu_{n}^{(k)})(h_{n}^{(k)})$}\hskip-35.03308pt\hskip-451.29065pt\raisebox{19.87904pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-3.34055pt}{\resizebox{53617400}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14236pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.98pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14285pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-451.29065pt\hskip 451.29065pt}\hskip 30.00005pt\text{[Conditional version of Eq.~\eqref{eq:error_noncond_factored}]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{L}\sum_{k=1}^{L}\int_{S}\lambda y.\rho_{k}(\widetilde{y}_{n}^{(k)},y)\;d((\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\delta_{x^{(i)}_{n}})\odot(q^{(k)}_{n}\cdot\upsilon_{S}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{L}\sum_{k=1}^{L}\int_{S}\lambda y.\rho_{k}(\widetilde{y}_{n}^{(k)},y)\;d\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\delta_{x^{(i)}_{n}}\odot(q^{(k)}_{n}\cdot\upsilon_{S}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{LN}\sum_{k=1}^{L}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{S}\lambda y.\rho_{k}(\widetilde{y}_{n}^{(k)},y)\;d(\delta_{x^{(i)}_{n}}\odot(q^{(k)}_{n}\cdot\upsilon_{S}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{LN}\sum_{k=1}^{L}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{S}\lambda y.\rho_{k}(\widetilde{y}_{n}^{(k)},y)\;d(q^{(k)}_{n}(x^{(i)}_{n})\cdot\upsilon_{S})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{LN}\sum_{k=1}^{L}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{S}\lambda y.\rho_{k}(\widetilde{y}_{n}^{(k)},y)\;q^{(k)}_{n}(x^{(i)}_{n})\;d\upsilon_{S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\;\frac{1}{LN}\sum_{k=1}^{L}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{S}\lambda y.\sum_{j=1}^{4}|\widetilde{y}_{n,j}^{(k)}-y_{j}|\;q^{(k)}_{n}(x^{(i)}_{n})\;d\upsilon_{S}.

In the derivation above,
\widetilde{y}_{n}^{(k)}=(\widetilde{y}_{n,1}^{(k)},\widetilde{y}_{n,2}^{(k)},\widetilde{y}_{n,3}^{(k)})
is the ground truth state for the factor
k
at time
n
from the simulation,

\widetilde{y}_{n,4}^{(k)}\triangleq 1-\sum_{i=1}^{3}\widetilde{y}_{n,i}^{(k)}
,

y=(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3})
, and
y_{4}\triangleq 1-\sum_{i=1}^{3}y_{i}
.
Also, if
h:X\rightarrow\mathcal{D}(Y)
is a conditional density and
\nu
a measure on
Y
,
then
h\cdot\nu:X\rightarrow\mathcal{P}(Y)
is the probability kernel defined by

h\cdot\nu=\lambda x.\lambda B.\int_{Y}\mathbf{1}_{B}\,h(x)\,d\nu
.
There is an analogous definition if
h:\mathcal{D}(Y)
is a density.
So
q^{(k)}_{n}\cdot\upsilon_{S}
is a probability kernel that has
q^{(k)}_{n}
as its corresponding conditional density.

Appendix F
Practical Considerations

Some practical considerations of applying the particle versions of the filtering algorithms, namely, Algorithms
2
,
5
,
8
, and
11
, are presented here.

In the standard particle filter (Algorithm
2
), the transition update involves sampling from a mixture of Dirac measures

\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)}\sim\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{N}\tau_{n}(h_{n-1},a_{n},y_{n-1}^{(i^{\prime})}),\text{ for }i=1,\dots,N.

Typically, this approach can be implemented by first sampling an index
i^{\prime}
from the set
\{1,\dots,N\}
uniformly at random, then applying the
n
th component of the transition model
\tau
to the particle
y_{n-1}^{(i^{\prime})}
(along with the history and action arguments).
The average-case running time of
sampling an index
is
O(1)
, irrespective of the value of
N
, using a typical pseudorandom number generator and a sampling method like the algorithm of ? (?);
the worst-case running time is
O(2^{W})
(
W
is the bit width of the computer, for example,
W=64
, for a 64-bit computer) which is constant time complexity for a fixed
W
.
But, in practice, an implementation such as

\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)}\sim\tau_{n}(h_{n-1},a_{n},y_{n-1}^{(i)}),\text{ for }i=1,\dots,N,

is usually more efficient and produces a particle family with lower variance (?) and thus is preferred.
Similar remarks apply to
Algorithms
5
,
8
, and
11
.

Another consideration with regard to the particle filtering algorithms is the resampling of particles.
Many resampling methods have been developed in the literature, as summarized by ? (?).
To preserve the diversity of the parameter particle family, we adopt a modified resampling approach (?) with respect to the effective sample size (ESS) (?, ?) of particles when Algorithm
2
is paired with Algorithms
10
,
11
, and
12
to estimate parameters.
In particular, the (normalized) weights of particles
(x_{n}^{(i)})_{i=1}^{N}
at time step
n
are

\overline{w}_{n}^{(i)}\propto(w_{n}^{(i)})^{\frac{1}{T}}\text{ for }i=1,\dots,N,

and we determine the value of
T
such that the estimated ESS of the particles

\widehat{\text{ESS}}((x_{n}^{(i)})_{i=1}^{N})=\frac{1}{\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\overline{w}_{n}^{(i)})^{2}},

is no less than a given threshold.
